## Status: WORK FILE (read lane R5 of `unified/UNIFICATION_RUN.md`; reference only, resolves nothing at runtime, CLAUDE.md §0.05; deleted when the unification stage closes)

**Lane.** `proposals/16_*`, `17_*`, `18_*`, `20_*`, `21_*`, `22_*`, `23_*` under `proposals/2026-11-12-subsystem-audit-suite/proposals/` (74 files: 16_ 42, 17_ 8, 18_ 4, 20_ 6, 21_ 5, 22_ 4, 23_ 5; 766,271 bytes by `wc -c`; resolved by `Glob` and `ls`. Lane-table check, by prefix over `ls proposals/`: every prefix 01-31 and `U5a`-`U5d` falls in exactly one lane, so no file is in none or in two). Every one of the 74 files was opened and read in full (the `Read` tool, whole file; none sampled). **Read first (done):** `CLAUDE.md`; `UNIFICATION_BRIEF.md`; `AUDIT_BRIEF.md` (head, standing rules, last four sections); `unified/DECISIONS_ANSWERED.md` (A-1 to A-91); `unified/OWED_TO_UNIFICATION.md`; `architecture/meta/04_CODE_ARCHITECTURE.md` in full; `holonic_ARCHITECTURE.md` §43-§47; `skills/layer-conformance/SKILL.md`; `audits/25_GROUNDING_ENGINE.md` in full; `unified/U1_CAPABILITY_AUTHORING_ARCHITECTURE.md` in full; `unified/U5_FINDINGS_CLOSURE_U5d.md` Parts 6, 8 to 13, R, "Jordan" and Coverage in full, and `unified/U5_CONFLICTS_U5c.md` C-15 and C-36 by grep (the other closure and conflict tables were not read; they concern other lanes' families).

**Read-only.** No proposal, audit, code, canon, registry or ruling was edited. Tree facts are `main` at the working tree (`git` shallow). Implementation lane A's two commits are unmerged and not read.

**Conventions.** `P:n` = the proposal's own edit number. `04:NNN` = line of `architecture/meta/04_CODE_ARCHITECTURE.md`. `[ASSUMPTION: ... — basis]` marks a guess. Short names drop the numeric prefix only in cross-references; row keys use the full file stem.

**Global findings (they recur across files and are stated once here).**
- **G-A. No proposal in this family names a model tier.** Test 9 lists "cost class and tier"; every file names a cost class (Edit / Small build / Design-first) and none names `haiku|sonnet|opus|fable`. Completing text is in 7b (one line per file, derived by CLAUDE.md §10: deterministic edit = sonnet; judgment content = opus).
- **G-B. Gates cite probe scripts that are not in the repository** (`knock.py`, `a0_words.py`, `c1_two_men.py`, `c2_stopped.py`, `req_forge.py`, `p34.py`, `run_dump.py`, ...). `git ls-files | grep` for each returns nothing; they lived in a lane scratch directory. A gate that cites them is not an execution artifact a later batch can run. Where the gate can be re-stated as an existing harness command (`python -m engine.season.harness.<x>`, `harness/` lists 30 modules) 7b does so; otherwise the script is listed under "blocked: the probe must be committed under `engine/season/harness/` or `tests/`" in 7.
- **G-C. Whole-dice figures.** Several bodies quote `band_odds` enumerations, Ob 1.0/2.0 tables and `obstacle_default` as live; A-5, A-6, A-7, A-26 and A-79 retire them. Heads say so ("Reconciled"), bodies do not follow. Listed per file in section 2.
- **G-D. "Event subject" wording.** Several files say an Event has a "subject" or an "actor"; 04 §B.9 `:433` says the fields do not exist (STRUCTURAL). Section 1 rows mark the wording; the structure (the receipt names the person) is what the rewrite lane writes.
- **G-E. `World.new_draw` ordinal draws** conflict with PART D 35 and A-30 (one draw per key). Files that rely on an ordinal or a draw position: `16_PESSIMIST_CHAR_A` ("the S33 ordinal", section 2) and `18_DETERMINISM_no_certain_matchup` ("the same draw position", section 1).

- **G-F. A head line governs its body in the reconciled files, and the body was not rewritten.** `unified/U5_FINDINGS_CLOSURE_U5d.md` (Coverage, `:935-937`) says so: "where a file's body holds a further passage built on the overruled answer, the head line governs it and the passage is not individually rewritten". Section 2 lists the passages in this family (about 40 rows), each with the edit.
- **G-G. Counts.** Section 1: 101 rows (72 CONFORMS, 19 AMBIGUOUS, 8 VIOLATES, 2 mixed); section 2: 95 rows; section 3: 163; section 4: 88; section 5: 74 (one per file); section 6: 74; section 7: 74 files, 8 COMPLETE (but for G-A) and 66 INCOMPLETE; section 7b: one block per decidable file.

**17_CENSUS_REST_2_wiring, W3 (rewritten edits 2-4 to the head's `capability_admits`).**

```python
# engine/season/queries/person_q.py  (pure; reads the hearer's own sheet)
def retainable(p, predicate: str, fx) -> bool:
    """May this mind hold a told claim of this predicate? `mundane`: always. `thread_substrate`: only where
    capability_admits(p, "told_retain", fx) is true (U1 s3 amendment 5; the one gate A-11 admits)."""
    kind = KNOWLEDGE_KIND_OF.get(predicate, "mundane")
    return kind == "mundane" or capability_admits(p, "told_retain", fx) is True
```

`capability_admits` returns `bool | UNKNOWN` (U1 §8): an UNKNOWN (an unauthored hearer) is not admitted, which keeps the existing behaviour "hearer_sensitivity is not None". `loop/witness.py`, told channel, before `tc = Claim(...)`: `if not person_q.retainable(w.persons[pid], _held.predicate, w.fixtures): continue`. `rosters.yaml: tables.capability_uses` row: `{use: told_retain, role: gate, key: prac.thread_sensitivity, side: subject}` (head). Tests: NEW `engine/season/tests/test_told_retain.py` beside `test_k3_thread_sensitivity.py`: `test_hearer_below_gate_keeps_the_mundane_claim_and_news_told_not_the_substrate_claim`, `test_hearer_at_gate_keeps_both` (`checked == 4`), `test_empty_substrate_list_gains_both` (the falsifier). Order: after `14_VERBS_PHYSICAL_D_thread_read.md` stage 1 and the U1 capability block. Layer 1 note for U2: WITNESS reading `Person.thread_sensitivity` (04 `:180` lists no Person field among its reads) is graded AMBIGUOUS in section 1; decision: keep as a retention rule at WITNESS through the one reader. Tier: opus for the placement call, sonnet for the edit.

**Layer 1 conflicts that survive the ladder (two options each, for Jordan).**
1. *Interior content at CENSUS* (`21_PROCEDURAL_B` (d), `21_PROCEDURAL_D` (g), `23_STORY_B` edit 3). 04 §A.2 `:179` licenses CENSUS (MATTER) to write `exists`, `weight`, `envelope`; pursuits, relations and seed claims are ACTS / INTERIOR cells (inv. 5, `:497`).
